# Breakthrough Plan: AMD E2E Model Speedrun — Autonomous K-Search Optimization

## Context

**Competition**: AMD E2E Model Speedrun | **Deadline**: March 30, 2026 (11 days)
**Problem**: 90+ Python parameter-tuning variants exhausted across 4+ months. Marginal gains only. Need architectural breakthrough.

| Kernel | Our Best | Leader | Gap |
|--------|----------|--------|-----|
| MoE | ~184 µs | 145 µs | 1.27x |
| GEMM | ~23 µs | 9.7 µs | 2.4x |
| MLA | ~67 µs | 4.3 µs | 15.6x |

**Core Strategy**: Synthesize K-Search (tree-structured optimization), R-Zero (adversarial co-evolution), and Autoresearch (autonomous experiment loop) into a unified system that runs overnight, generating and benchmarking kernel variants on the actual MI355X runner via popcorn-cli.

## Key Untried Levers (from Research)

1. **MLA `num_kv_splits` is hardcoded to 32 everywhere** — optimal value varies 16x by shape (bs=4 → splits=4, bs=256 → splits=64+). This alone could close half the MLA gap.
2. **MLA MXFP4 KV path** — runner provides it, reference uses FP8, nobody has tried MXFP4 (2x bandwidth reduction over FP8)
3. **GEMM exhaustive per-shape lookup** — 6 shapes × {ASM kernel variants} × {split-K 0-4}. Grid search on runner (not local)
4. **MoE per-shape KSPLIT table** — 7 shapes with 2.5x variance. Optimize worst shapes for geomean impact

---

## Architecture: K-Search Autonomous Loop

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                  driver.py (main loop)               │
│                                                      │
│  ┌──────────┐  ┌────────────┐  ┌──────────────────┐ │
│  │ K-Search │→ │ Template   │→ │ popcorn-cli      │ │
│  │ Tree     │  │ Generator  │  │ test → benchmark │ │
│  │ SELECT   │  │ (Ollama)   │  │ → leaderboard    │ │
│  └────┬─────┘  └────────────┘  └────────┬─────────┘ │
│       │                                  │           │
│       └──── UPDATE/INSERT/PRUNE ←────────┘           │
│             (results analysis)                       │
│                                                      │
│  Rate limiter: 1 leaderboard/hr/problem              │
│  Budget: ~75 cycles/night (8 min/cycle)              │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

### Files to Create

```
research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/
├── driver.py              # Main overnight experiment loop
├── ksearch_tree.py        # K-Search tree (KNode dataclass, SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE)

## Implementation: 4 Phases over 11 Days

### Phase 1: Infrastructure + Baselines (Day 1-2)

**Task 1.1**: Create `ksearch_tree.py`
- `KNode` dataclass: id, strategy, parent_id, priority (0-1), attempts, best_result_us, stagnation_count, status, parameters, children, notes
- Operations: `select_best()` → highest-priority active node, `insert_child()`, `update_priority()`, `prune_exhausted()` (K=7 stagnation threshold)
- Persistence: JSON load/save

**Task 1.2**: Create parameterized templates
- GEMM: `{shape_thresholds: [int×4], kernel_names: [str×5], log2_k_splits: [int×5]}`
- MoE: `{ksplit_table: {shape_key: int}, use_opus: bool, bypass_tune: bool}`
- MLA: `{splits_table: {(bs,kv): int}, fast_mode: bool, kv_dtype: "fp8"|"mxfp4", kv_granularity: int}`
- Templates are Python string.Template with `$PARAM` substitution — Ollama/Claude outputs JSON params, not code

**Task 1.3**: Create `evaluator.py`
- Wraps: `~/.local/bin/popcorn-cli submit --no-tui --mode {test|benchmark|leaderboard} --gpu MI355X --leaderboard {name} {path}`
- Parses output for: pass/fail, per-shape timings, geomean, correctness errors
- Handles: transient artifact failures (retry once), timeout (12 min cap)

**Task 1.4**: Create `driver.py` — the autoresearch loop
- SELECT node → generate submission from template → write file → popcorn-cli test → if pass: benchmark → UPDATE tree → log to JSONL → sleep 5s → repeat
- Rate limiter: track last leaderboard timestamp per problem, gate to 1/hr
- Rotation: cycle through MoE → GEMM → MLA (priority-weighted)

**Task 1.5**: Establish REAL baselines on runner
- Submit current `submission.py` for all 3 kernels in test+benchmark mode
- Get actual per-shape timings (not estimates from staging)
- Identify which shapes are bottlenecks for geomean

### Phase 2: MoE Sprint (Day 3-5) — Priority 1

**Why first**: 1.27x gap is closable with parameter tuning. No architectural change needed.

**Pre-seeded K-Search tree for MoE**:
```
root: "Minimize fused_moe geomean across 7 shapes"
├── node_1: "Per-shape KSPLIT lookup table" (p=0.95)
│   ├── 1a: "E=257,d=256,bs=16: KSPLIT=4" (p=0.9)
│   ├── 1b: "E=257,d=256,bs=64: KSPLIT=6" (p=0.85)
│   ├── 1c: "E=33,d=512,bs=128: KSPLIT=2" (p=0.8)
│   ├── 1d: "E=33,d=2048,bs=512: KSPLIT=0" (p=0.75)
│   └── 1e: "E=33,d=256,bs=32: KSPLIT=3" (p=0.7)
├── node_2: "OPUS sorting + KSPLIT combo" (p=0.7)
├── node_3: "BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG=1 + manual KSPLIT" (p=0.65)
├── node_4: "Direct torch.ops.aiter.fmoe bypassing Python" (p=0.5)
└── node_5: "block_size_M tuning (32, 64, 128)" (p=0.4)
```

**Approach**: Run 15-20 MoE variants through test+benchmark. Each variant is a different KSPLIT table. Identify which of the 7 shapes responds most to KSPLIT changes. Focus on worst shapes (highest absolute time) since they dominate geomean.

**Target**: 150-155 µs geomean → Top 5

### Phase 3: GEMM Sprint (Day 5-7) — Priority 2

**Pre-seeded K-Search tree for GEMM**:
```
root: "Minimize MXFP4 GEMM geomean across 6 shapes"
├── node_1: "Per-shape ASM kernel + split-K table" (p=0.95)
│   ├── 1a: "M=4,N=2880,K=512: 32x128 ks=4" (p=0.9)
│   ├── 1b: "M=16,N=2112,K=7168: 32x128 ks=3" (p=0.85)
│   ├── 1c: "M=32,N=4096,K=512: 128x128 ks=1" (p=0.8)
│   ├── 1d: "M=32,N=2880,K=512: 32x256 ks=2" (p=0.75)
│   ├── 1e: "M=64,N=7168,K=2048: 192x128 ks=0" (p=0.7)
│   └── 1f: "M=256,N=3072,K=1536: 256x128 ks=0" (p=0.65)
├── node_2: "gemm_a4w4_asm vs gemm_a4w4 per shape" (p=0.85)
├── node_3: "N-dependent tile selection" (p=0.6)
└── node_4: "Skip quant for pre-quantized B (bypass dynamic_mxfp4_quant)" (p=0.5)
```

**Key experiment**: Submit 6 single-shape probes to isolate per-shape reference timing. Then build exhaustive lookup table: for each shape, test all valid (kernel_name, log2_ks) combos. There are ~16 tile configs × 5 split-K values = 80 combos per shape, but most are invalid for small M.

**Target**: 12-15 µs geomean → Top 10

### Phase 4: MLA Moonshot (Day 7-10) — Priority 3

**Pre-seeded K-Search tree for MLA**:
```
root: "Minimize MLA decode geomean across 8 shapes"
├── node_1: "Adaptive num_kv_splits per (bs, kvseqlen)" (p=0.95) ★ HIGHEST LEVERAGE
│   ├── 1a: "bs=4,kv=1024: splits=4" (p=0.9)
│   ├── 1b: "bs=4,kv=8192: splits=16" (p=0.85)
│   ├── 1c: "bs=32,kv=1024: splits=8" (p=0.8)
│   ├── 1d: "bs=32,kv=8192: splits=32" (p=0.75)
│   ├── 1e: "bs=64,kv=1024: splits=16" (p=0.7)
│   ├── 1f: "bs=64,kv=8192: splits=32" (p=0.65)
│   ├── 1g: "bs=256,kv=1024: splits=32" (p=0.6)
│   └── 1h: "bs=256,kv=8192: splits=64" (p=0.55)
├── node_2: "MXFP4 KV cache (2x bandwidth vs FP8)" (p=0.85) ★ UNTRIED
├── node_3: "fast_mode=True metadata" (p=0.7)
├── node_4: "kv_granularity tuning (16/32/64)" (p=0.6)
├── node_5: "Pre-compute metadata outside timed region" (p=0.5)
└── node_6: "Custom Triton reduce kernel for split-K output" (p=0.3)
```

**The big bet — adaptive num_kv_splits**: The formula from program.md has never been properly tested on the runner:
```python
# For each (bs, kvseqlen) pair, find optimal num_kv_splits:
# minimize: (bs * i) / ceil_cu(bs * i) * avg_kv / (avg_kv + 84.1 * i)
# where ceil_cu(x) = ceil(x / 304) * 304
```
This alone could move from 67 µs to 30-40 µs.

**The MXFP4 KV experiment**: The runner provides `kv_data_dict["mxfp4"]` but nobody has used it. If `mla_decode_fwd` accepts it, that's 2x bandwidth reduction. Submit one probe to test.

**Target**: 25-40 µs → Top 10 (moonshot: <20 µs)

### Day 11: Final Push

- Submit best variants for all 3 kernels to leaderboard
- Run one final overnight autonomous loop focused on closest-to-winning kernel
- Save all results to vault

## K-Search ↔ R-Zero ↔ Autoresearch Mapping

| Concept | K-Search | R-Zero | Autoresearch | Our System |
|---------|----------|--------|--------------|------------|
| Search structure | Optimization tree | Challenger curriculum | Linear experiments | K-Search tree per kernel |
| Generator | LLM code gen | Challenger agent | Agent edits train.py | Template + Ollama JSON params |
| Evaluator | Execution trace | Solver attempts | val_bpb metric | popcorn-cli benchmark geomean |
| Evolution | Insert/Update/Prune | Co-evolution loop | Keep/discard | Tree ops + stagnation pruning |
| Stagnation | K=7 threshold | Difficulty escalation | Autonomous overnight | K=7 + priority decay |

## Dead Ends (DO NOT RETRY)

- `doweight_stage1=True` — 82% mismatch or GPU crash (AITER bug)
- `torch.compile(fused_moe)` — assertion crash on ROCm 7.1
- Pure Triton kernels — 50-70% slower than CK/ASM
- hiprtc compilation — blocked by runner
- `expert_mask=bincount` — GPU memory fault
- `num_kv_splits=64+` — exceeds aiter limits
- `AITER_JIT_DIR` pre-caching — internal error 1

1. All 3 kernels pass `--mode test` on runner before any benchmark
2. Benchmark geomean matches or improves current best before leaderboard submission
3. Results logged to `autoresearch/results/*.jsonl` with full per-shape timing
4. K-Search trees updated after every experiment cycle
5. Best submissions staged via COORDINATION.md protocol before leaderboard
6. Final verification: submit to leaderboard, confirm score matches benchmark

## All 3 Submission Files (Complete Content)

### `kernels/moe-mxfp4/submission.py`

14 lines, heavily minified. Uses `aiter.fused_moe` with adaptive KSPLIT strategy:
- KSPLIT="0" (default CSV tuning) when estimated_m >= 50
- KSPLIT="4" when num_experts >= 200 and estimated_m < 10
- KSPLIT="2" otherwise
- Sets `AITER_USE_NT=1`, `AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG=1`
- Calls `fused_moe` with `doweight_stage1=False`, `quant_type=qt.per_1x32`, uses shuffled weights/scales

### `kernels/mxfp4-mm/submission.py`

38 lines, clean readable code. Uses `aiter.gemm_a4w4`:
- Quantizes A from bf16 to MXFP4 using `dynamic_mxfp4_quant`
- Shuffles A scales with `e8m0_shuffle`
- Calls `aiter.gemm_a4w4` with `bpreshuffle=True` on pre-shuffled B

### `kernels/mixed-mla/submission.py`

34 lines, minified. Uses aiter MLA persistent ASM kernel:
- Sets `AITER_MLA_USE_PERSISTENT=1`, `AITER_USE_NT=1`, `AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG=1`
- Eagerly loads `mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd` and `mla_reduce_v1`
- Caches metadata per (batch_size * kv_seq_len) key
- Adaptive num_kv_splits: 16 if seq_len > 4096, else 8
- Pre-allocates output buffers with caching
- Uses MXFP4 KV cache format (fp4x2 + scale)
- Falls back to `mla_decode_fwd` if ASM functions unavailable

## COORDINATION.md Files

### Root `COORDINATION.md`

Tracks multi-session coordination with staging directory workflow. Current gaps to leaders:
- GEMM: ~23us current, ~9.7us leader (2.4x gap)
- MLA: ~67us current, ~4.3us leader (15.6x gap)
- MoE: ~184us current, ~145us leader (1.27x gap)

Defines naming convention `submission.<session>.<timestamp>.py`, lock protocol for Popcorn CLI submissions, and consensus rules (2+ sessions agree before promoting).

### `kernels/mixed-mla/COORDINATION.md`

MLA-specific optimization log. Documents attempt history:
- Attempt 1: Direct reference delegation (timed out due to JIT)
- Attempt 2: Custom HIP kernel with MXFP4 (current)
- Key insight: aiter 3-stage pipeline has ~100-150us fixed overhead; leaders at 4.3us likely use fused Flash Attention
- `fast_mode=False` is paradoxically faster on MI355X

### `~/.claude/skills/k-search-llm-kernel-optimization/SKILL.md`

Version 1.0.0. Based on arxiv 2602.19128 (UC Berkeley). Documents:
- Two-role architecture: pi_plan (world model/search tree) vs pi_code (implementation)
- 3-phase iteration: Action Selection, Program Instantiation (K=7 stagnation limit), World Model Co-Evolution (Insert/Update/Prune)
- Results: 14.3x over OpenEvolve on MoE, 2.1x average across all kernels
- Discovered MoE strategies: warp-level routing, token reordering, tensor core WMMA, expert skipping
- Current Luma MoE search tree: parameter tuning CLOSED, custom Triton CLOSED; active-expert masking (V=0.7), direct CK dispatch (V=0.5), torch.compile (V=0.4) are OPEN
- Key lessons: non-monotonic paths are normal, implementation defects are not strategy failures, prune aggressively

     1→#!POPCORN leaderboard amd-mxfp4-mm
     2→#!POPCORN gpu MI355X
     3→
     4→"""
     5→GEMM Submission: Optimized MXFP4 GEMM using aiter gemm_a4w4.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→import torch
     9→from task import input_t, output_t
    10→from aiter import dtypes
    11→import aiter
    12→from aiter.ops.triton.quant import dynamic_mxfp4_quant
    13→from aiter.utility.fp4_utils import e8m0_shuffle
    14→
    15→
    16→def custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:
    17→    """
    18→    MXFP4 GEMM: A (bf16) x B (MXFP4) -> C (bf16)
    19→    """
    20→    A, B, B_q, B_shuffle, B_scale_sh = data
    21→    A = A.contiguous()
    22→
    23→    # Quantize A to MXFP4 with shuffled scales
    24→    A_q_raw, A_scale_raw = dynamic_mxfp4_quant(A)
    25→    A_scale_shuffled = e8m0_shuffle(A_scale_raw).view(dtypes.fp8_e8m0)
    26→    A_q = A_q_raw.view(dtypes.fp4x2)
    27→
    28→    # Call aiter GEMM kernel with bpreshuffle=True
    29→    C = aiter.gemm_a4w4(
    30→        A_q,
    31→        B_shuffle,
    32→        A_scale_shuffled,
    33→        B_scale_sh,
    34→        dtype=dtypes.bf16,
    35→        bpreshuffle=True,
    36→    )
    37→
    38→    return C
    39→

     1→import os,torch,ctypes;from task import input_t,output_t;from aiter import dtypes as dt;import aiter
     2→os.environ["AITER_MLA_USE_PERSISTENT"]="1";os.environ["AITER_USE_NT"]="1";os.environ["AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG"]="1"
     3→_c,_o,_f1,_f2={},{},None,None
     4→def _ea():
     5→ global _f1,_f2;if _f1:return
     6→ try:
     7→  from aiter.mla import mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd as f1,mla_reduce_v1 as f2
     8→  _f1,_f2=f1,f2
     9→ except:
    10→  import aiter as a
    11→  _f1,_f2=getattr(a,"mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd",None),getattr(a,"mla_reduce_v1",None)
    12→def _bm(b,q,n,qd,kd,qi,ki,ns):
    13→ from aiter import get_mla_metadata_info_v1 as gi,get_mla_metadata_v1 as gv
    14→ i=gi(b,q,n,qd,kd,is_sparse=False,fast_mode=False,num_kv_splits=ns,intra_batch_mode=True)
    15→ w=[torch.empty(s,dtype=t,device="cuda") for s,t in i];wm,wi,ws,ri,rf,rp=w
    16→ kl=(ki[1:]-ki[:-1]).to(torch.int32)
    17→ gv(qi,ki,kl,n,1,True,wm,ws,wi,ri,rf,rp,page_size=1,kv_granularity=16,max_seqlen_qo=q,uni_seqlen_qo=q,fast_mode=False,max_split_per_batch=ns,intra_batch_mode=True,dtype_q=qd,dtype_kv=kd)
    18→ return {"wm":wm,"wi":wi,"ws":ws,"ri":ri,"rf":rf,"rp":rp,"kl":kl}
    19→def custom_kernel(data:input_t)->output_t:
    20→ q,kd,qi,ki,cfg=data;b,sl,nh=cfg["batch_size"],cfg["kv_seq_len"],cfg["num_heads"];_ea()
    21→ if(b*sl)not in _c:
    22→  ns=16 if sl>4096 else 8
    23→  _c[b*sl]=_bm(b,1,nh,torch.bfloat16,dt.fp4x2,qi,ki,ns)
    24→ m=_c[b*sl];ok=(b*nh,512)
    25→ if ok not in _o:_o[ok]=torch.empty((b,nh,512),dtype=torch.bfloat16,device="cuda")
    26→ ot=_o[ok];kf,ks=kd["mxfp4"];k4=kf.view(kf.shape[0],1,1,288)

    28→ if _f1 and _f2:
    29→  sd=torch.empty((b,16,nh,520),dtype=torch.float32,device="cuda")
    30→  sl_=torch.empty((b,16,nh),dtype=torch.float32,device="cuda")
    31→  _f1(q.view(b,nh,576),k4,qi,ki,torch.arange(b*sl,dtype=torch.int32,device="cuda"),m["kl"],None,m["wm"],m["wi"],m["ws"],1,1,1,1.0/(576**.5),sd,sl_,ot,q_scale=None,kv_scale=ks)
    32→  _f2(sd,sl_,m["ri"],m["rf"],m["rp"],1,ot);return ot
    33→ from aiter.mla import mla_decode_fwd as mf
    34→ return mf(q,k4,ot,qi,ki,page_size=1,nhead_kv=1,sm_scale=1.0/(576**.5),q_scale=None,kv_scale=ks,num_kv_splits=16,intra_batch_mode=True)

     1→# name: 3_moe_mxfp4
     2→
     3→files:
     4→  - {"name": "submission.py", "source": "@SUBMISSION@"}
     5→  - {"name": "task.py", "source": "task.py"}
     6→  - {"name": "utils.py", "source": "../utils.py"}
     7→  - {"name": "reference.py", "source": "reference.py"}
     8→  - {"name": "eval.py", "source": "../eval.py"}
     9→
    10→lang: "py"
    11→
    12→description: |
    13→  You will implement a DeepSeek-R1 style MXFP4 Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) fused kernel optimized for AMD Instinct MI355X GPU.
    14→
    15→  To be explicit, you will be given a tuple of tensors:
    16→  ```
    17→  (hidden_states,
    18→   gate_up_weight, down_weight,                                         # fp4x2 raw
    19→   gate_up_weight_scale, down_weight_scale,                             # e8m0  raw
    20→   gate_up_weight_shuffled, down_weight_shuffled,                       # fp4x2 pre-shuffled
    21→   gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled, down_weight_scale_shuffled,           # e8m0  pre-shuffled
    22→   topk_weights, topk_ids,
    23→   config)
    24→  ```
    25→  where:
    26→  * `hidden_states` is M x d_hidden in bfloat16 (the input activations, M = batch of tokens)
    27→  * `gate_up_weight` is [E, 2*d_expert_pad, d_hidden_pad//2] in MXFP4 (fp4x2), raw layout.
    28→    Fused gate + up projection weights for each expert. E = number of local experts.
    29→  * `down_weight` is [E, d_hidden_pad, d_expert_pad//2] in MXFP4 (fp4x2), raw layout.
    30→    Down projection weights for each expert.
    31→  * `gate_up_weight_scale` is [E, 2*d_expert_pad, d_hidden_pad//32] in E8M0, raw layout.
    32→    Block scales (block_size=32) for gate_up_weight.
    33→  * `down_weight_scale` is [E, d_hidden_pad, d_expert_pad//32] in E8M0, raw layout.
    34→    Block scales for down_weight.
    35→  * `gate_up_weight_shuffled` / `down_weight_shuffled` are the same weights shuffled to
    36→    (16,16) tile-coalesced layout for the CK kernel.
    37→  * `gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled` / `down_weight_scale_shuffled` are the scales after
    38→    e8m0_shuffle, flattened to [padded, flat].
    39→  * `topk_weights` is [M, total_top_k] float32: routing weights (routed experts + shared experts).
    40→  * `topk_ids` is [M, total_top_k] int32: expert indices. First nexpertspertoken columns are
    41→    routed expert ids (0..n_routed-1), last nsharedexperts columns are shared expert ids
    42→    (n_routed..n_routed+n_shared-1). Shared experts are always selected with weight=1.0.
    43→  * `config` is a dict with: d_hidden, d_expert, d_hidden_pad, d_expert_pad,
    44→    n_routed_experts, n_shared_experts, n_experts_per_token, total_top_k, bs.
    45→
    46→  Then, the fused_moe kernel flow is:
    47→  (1) Quant activations to MXFP4: aiter per-1x32 dynamic quantization of hidden_states.
    48→  (2) Stage 1 GEMM + activation (per token i, per assigned expert j):
    49→      - gate = x_i @ W_gate_j.T          # [d_hidden] x [d_expert, d_hidden].T -> [d_expert]
    50→      - up   = x_i @ W_up_j.T            # [d_hidden] x [d_expert, d_hidden].T -> [d_expert]
    51→      - intermediate = SiLU(gate) * up    # SwiGLU activation, -> [d_expert]
    52→      (W_gate and W_up are fused as gate_up_weight, so this is one a4w4 GEMM + fused activation)
    53→  (3) Stage 2 GEMM:
    54→      - expert_out = intermediate @ W_down_j.T  # [d_expert] x [d_hidden, d_expert].T -> [d_hidden]
    55→  (4) Weighted reduction:
    56→      - output_i += w_ij * expert_out     # accumulate across top_k experts
    57→  All weight GEMMs are a4w4 (MXFP4 activations x MXFP4 weights, per-1x32 block scaling).
    58→  The AITER CK kernel fuses all of the above into a 2-stage pipeline across all tokens and experts.
    59→
    60→  DeepSeek-R1 MoE specs:
    61→    - hidden_size = 7168, moe_intermediate_size = 2048
    62→    - 256 routed experts + 1 shared expert (total 257), top-8 routed + 1 shared = 9 per token
    63→    - 58 MoE layers (layer 3-60)
    64→    - The shared expert processes ALL tokens unconditionally (weight=1.0)
    65→
    66→  d_hidden_pad and d_expert_pad are the dimensions padded to 256-alignment for the CK kernel.
    67→
    68→  The ranking criteria is the geometric mean of the benchmark results.
    69→
    70→  ```
    71→  The AITER reference performance is (E includes shared expert, top_k = routed + shared):
    72→    bs     E  d_hidden  d_expert  top_k  time[us]
    73→    16   257      7168       256      9    152.7
    74→   128   257      7168       256      9    239.0
    75→   512   257      7168       256      9    336.5
    76→    16    33      7168       512      9    106.2
    77→   128    33      7168       512      9    141.1
    78→   512    33      7168       512      9    225.0
    79→   512    33      7168      2048      9    380.4
    80→  ```
    81→
    82→  Input:
    83→    - hidden_states:                  [M, d_hidden]                          bf16
    84→    - gate_up_weight:                 [E, 2*d_expert_pad, d_hidden_pad//2]   fp4x2 (raw, before shuffle)
    85→    - down_weight:                    [E, d_hidden_pad, d_expert_pad//2]     fp4x2 (raw, before shuffle)
    86→    - gate_up_weight_scale:           [E, 2*d_expert_pad, d_hidden_pad//32]  e8m0  (raw, before shuffle)
    87→    - down_weight_scale:              [E, d_hidden_pad, d_expert_pad//32]    e8m0  (raw, before shuffle)
    88→    - gate_up_weight_shuffled:        [E, 2*d_expert_pad, d_hidden_pad//2]   fp4x2 (pre-shuffled for CK)
    89→    - down_weight_shuffled:           [E, d_hidden_pad, d_expert_pad//2]     fp4x2 (pre-shuffled for CK)
    90→    - gate_up_weight_scale_shuffled:  [padded, flat]                         e8m0  (pre-shuffled for CK)
    91→    - down_weight_scale_shuffled:     [padded, flat]                         e8m0  (pre-shuffled for CK)
    92→    - topk_weights:                   [M, total_top_k]                       float32
    93→    - topk_ids:                       [M, total_top_k]                       int32
    94→    - config:                         dict with dimensions
    95→
    96→  Output:
    97→    - output: [M, d_hidden] bf16
    98→
    99→config:
   100→  main: "eval.py"
   101→
   102→templates:
   103→  Python: "submission.py"
   104→
   105→test_timeout: 540
   106→benchmark_timeout: 540
   107→ranked_timeout: 840
   108→ranking_by: "geom"
   109→

     1→# name: mla-py
     2→
     3→files:
     4→  - {"name": "submission.py", "source": "@SUBMISSION@"}
     5→  - {"name": "task.py", "source": "task.py"}
     6→  - {"name": "utils.py", "source": "../utils.py"}
     7→  - {"name": "reference.py", "source": "reference.py"}
     8→  - {"name": "eval.py", "source": "../eval.py"}
     9→
    10→lang: "py"
    11→
    12→description: |
    13→  Implement a custom MLA (Multi-head Latent Attention) decode kernel optimized for MI355X.
    14→
    15→  This is the inner attention kernel from DeepSeek R1's forward_absorb MLA path.
    16→  The absorbed query and compressed KV cache are provided directly — you only need to
    17→  implement the **attention** computation with variable-length batching (indptr).
    18→
    19→  The reference uses aiter a8w8 MLA decode kernel (mla_decode_fwd, fp8 Q + fp8 KV,
    20→  persistent mode), which is ~2-3x faster than bf16 on MI355X.
    21→
    22→  DeepSeek R1 forward_absorb MLA config:
    23→    - total_num_heads = 128 (query heads before TP split)
    24→    - num_heads = 128 // tp (query heads per device, tp=4 → 32, tp=8 → 16)
    25→    - num_kv_heads = 1 (shared latent KV head)
    26→    - kv_lora_rank = 512
    27→    - qk_rope_head_dim = 64
    28→    - qk_head_dim = 576 (kv_lora_rank + qk_rope_head_dim, absorbed q/k dim)
    29→    - v_head_dim = 512 (= kv_lora_rank, output dim)
    30→    - sm_scale = 1/sqrt(576)
    31→    - dtype: q=bfloat16
    32→    - q_seq_len = 1 or 4, kv_seq_len up to 8k
    33→
    34→  KV buffer format (forward_absorb):
    35→    - Full 576 dims are used as keys (for Q@K^T score computation)
    36→    - First 512 dims (kv_lora_rank) are used as values (for output computation)
    37→
    38→  Input tuple: (q, kv_data, qo_indptr, kv_indptr, config)
    39→    - q: (total_q, num_heads, 576) bfloat16 — absorbed query
    40→    - kv_data: dict with three KV cache formats:
    41→        kv_data["bf16"]  — Tensor (total_kv, 1, 576) bfloat16
    42→        kv_data["fp8"]   — (Tensor, Tensor): kv_buffer fp8 + scalar scale
    43→        kv_data["mxfp4"] — (Tensor, Tensor): kv_buffer fp4x2 + fp8_e8m0 scale
    44→    - qo_indptr: (batch_size+1,) int32 — query segment pointers
    45→    - kv_indptr: (batch_size+1,) int32 — KV segment pointers
    46→    - config: dict with MLA parameters (includes num_heads computed from tp)
    47→
    48→  Return:
    49→    - attention output: (total_q, num_heads, 512) bfloat16
    50→
    51→  Key optimization opportunities:
    52→    1. Use mxfp4 KV cache for even lower memory bandwidth (4x savings over bf16)
    53→       - Fuse dequantization with attention to skip bf16 materialization
    54→    2. Custom kernel with tighter memory access patterns
    55→    3. MQA: 1 KV head shared across num_heads query heads — minimize redundant memory loads
    56→    4. q_seq_len=1 or 4, kv_seq_len up to 8k — memory-bound workload
    57→    5. Variable-length batching: indptr-based segmented attention
    58→    6. Split K/V from buffer: full 576 dims for keys, first 512 dims for values
    59→
    60→  The ranking criteria is the geometric mean of the benchmark results.
    61→
    62→config:
    63→  main: "eval.py"
    64→
    65→templates:
    66→  Python: "submission.py"
    67→
    68→test_timeout: 900
    69→benchmark_timeout: 900
    70→ranked_timeout: 1200
    71→
    72→tests:
    73→  # bs=4, tp=8
    74→  - {"batchsize": 4, "qseqlen": 1, "kvseqlen": 1024, "tp": 8, "seed": 4220}
    75→  - {"batchsize": 4, "qseqlen": 4, "kvseqlen": 1024, "tp": 8, "seed": 4231}
    76→  # bs=32, tp=4
    77→  - {"batchsize": 32, "qseqlen": 1, "kvseqlen": 1024, "tp": 4, "seed": 5412}
    78→  - {"batchsize": 32, "qseqlen": 4, "kvseqlen": 8192, "tp": 4, "seed": 5423}
    79→  # bs=128, tp=8
    80→  - {"batchsize": 128, "qseqlen": 1, "kvseqlen": 8192, "tp": 8, "seed": 7816}
    81→  - {"batchsize": 128, "qseqlen": 4, "kvseqlen": 8192, "tp": 4, "seed": 7827}
    82→
    83→benchmarks:
    84→  # bs=4, tp=4
    85→  - {"batchsize": 4, "qseqlen": 1, "kvseqlen": 1024, "tp": 4, "seed": 4237}
    86→  - {"batchsize": 4, "qseqlen": 4, "kvseqlen": 8192, "tp": 4, "seed": 4251}
    87→  # bs=32, tp=8
    88→  - {"batchsize": 32, "qseqlen": 1, "kvseqlen": 8192, "tp": 8, "seed": 5415}
    89→  - {"batchsize": 32, "qseqlen": 4, "kvseqlen": 1024, "tp": 8, "seed": 5420}
    90→  # bs=32, tp=4

    10→lang: "py"
    11→
    12→description: |
    13→  You will implement a quantize func and block scaled MXFP4 matrix-matrix multiplication kernel optimized for AMD Instinct MI355X GPU.
    14→  To be explicit, you will be given a tuple of tensors:
    15→  ```
    16→  (A, B, B_q, B_shuffle, B_scale_sh)
    17→  ```
    18→  where:
    19→  * `A` is M x K in K-major order in bfloat16
    20→  * `B` is N x K in K-major order in bfloat16
    21→  * `B_q` is N x K/2 in K-major order in MXFP4
    22→  * `B_shuffle` is N x K/2 in shuffled order in MXFP4, shuffled to (16,16) tile coalesced
    23→  * `B_scale_sh` is * x K/32 in E8M0, * means it will be padded.
    24→
    25→  Then, the kernel flow is bf16 A, MXFP4 B -> MXFP4 per-1x32 quant A -> gemm_a4w4 -> BF16 C [m,n].
    26→  To be specific, the invocation flow is:
    27→  (1) Quant A to MXFP4: aiter.get_triton_quant(QuantType.per_1x32). 
    28→  (2) GEMM: aiter.gemm_a4w4.
    29→  m, n divisible by 64; k divisible by 64.
    30→
    31→  The ranking criteria is the geometric mean of the benchmark results.
    32→  Pls note that this is the elimination round, whoever rank top5 are selected into the next round, e2e optimization for deepseek-R1-MXFP4 and GPTOSS-MXFP4 mdoel

> AGENT

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**K-Search Tree Design**: The tree is a Monte Carlo Tree Search variant adapted for kernel optimization. Each node represents a "strategy" (e.g., "try KSPLIT=4 for shape X"). The SELECT/UPDATE/PRUNE cycle mirrors MCTS's explore-exploit tradeoff, but with a stagnation counter (K=7) instead of UCB scores — after 7 attempts without improvement, we prune that branch and explore elsewhere.

**Why JSON persistence**: The tree must survive across popcorn-cli cycles (8 min each). JSONL for results gives append-only logging; JSON for tree state gives atomic snapshot/restore.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/ksearch_tree.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"K-Search tree for kernel optimization.\n\nTree-structured search over kernel parameter configurations.\nEach node is a strategy (e.g., \"KSPLIT=4 for shape X\") with priority,\nattempt tracking, and stagnation-based pruning (K=7).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field, asdict\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\n\nSTAGNATION_THRESHOLD = 7  # Prune after 7 attempts without improvement\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass KNode:\n    id: str\n    strategy: str\n    parameters: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    parent_id: str | None = None\n    priority: float = 0.5  # 0.0 (low) to 1.0 (high)\n    attempts: int = 0\n    best_result_us: float | None = None\n    stagnation_count: int = 0\n    status: str = \"active\"  # active | exhausted | pruned\n    children: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n    notes: str = \"\"\n\n    def is_selectable(self) -> bool:\n        return self.status == \"active\"\n\n\nclass KSearchTree:\n    \"\"\"K-Search optimization tree with SELECT/INSERT/UPDATE/PRUNE.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, kernel_name: str):\n        self.kernel_name = kernel_name\n        self.nodes: dict[str, KNode] = {}\n        self.root_id: str | None = None\n\n    def insert_root(self, strategy: str, parameters: dict | None = None) -> KNode:\n        node = KNode(\n            id=f\"root_{self.kernel_name}\",\n            strategy=strategy,\n            parameters=parameters or {},\n            priority=1.0,\n        )\n        self.nodes[node.id] = node\n        self.root_id = node.id\n        return node\n\n    def insert_child(\n        self,\n        parent_id: str,\n        strategy: str,\n        parameters: dict[str, Any],\n        priority: float = 0.5,\n        notes: str = \"\",\n    ) -> KNode:\n        if parent_id not in self.nodes:\n            raise KeyError(f\"Parent {parent_id} not found\")\n        node_id = f\"n_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}\"\n        node = KNode(\n            id=node_id,\n            strategy=strategy,\n            parameters=parameters,\n            parent_id=parent_id,\n            priority=priority,\n            notes=notes,\n        )\n        self.nodes[node_id] = node\n        self.nodes[parent_id].children.append(node_id)\n        return node\n\n    def select_best(self) -> KNode | None:\n        \"\"\"Select highest-priority active node (leaf-first, then internal).\"\"\"\n        active = [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.is_selectable()]\n        if not active:\n            return None\n        # Prefer leaves (no active children) over internal nodes\n        leaves = [n for n in active if not any(\n            self.nodes.get(c, KNode(id=\"\")).is_selectable() for c in n.children\n        )]\n        candidates = leaves if leaves else active\n        return max(candidates, key=lambda n: n.priority)\n\n    def update_result(self, node_id: str, result_us: float) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Update node with benchmark result. Tracks improvement/stagnation.\"\"\"\n        node = self.nodes[node_id]\n        node.attempts += 1\n\n        if node.best_result_us is None or result_us < node.best_result_us:\n            improvement = (\n                (node.best_result_us - result_us) / node.best_result_us\n                if node.best_result_us\n                else 1.0\n            )\n            node.best_result_us = result_us\n            node.stagnation_count = 0\n            # Boost priority on improvement\n            node.priority = min(1.0, node.priority + 0.1 * improvement)\n        else:\n            node.stagnation_count += 1\n\n        # Auto-prune on stagnation\n        if node.stagnation_count >= STAGNATION_THRESHOLD:\n            node.status = \"exhausted\"\n            node.notes += f\" [Exhausted after {node.attempts} attempts]\"\n\n    def mark_failed(self, node_id: str, reason: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Mark node as pruned due to failure (correctness, crash, etc.).\"\"\"\n        node = self.nodes[node_id]\n        node.status = \"pruned\"\n        node.notes += f\" [PRUNED: {reason}]\"\n\n    def decay_priorities(self, factor: float = 0.95) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Decay all active node priorities to encourage exploration.\"\"\"\n        for node in self.nodes.values():\n            if node.is_selectable():\n                node.priority *= factor\n\n    def get_stats(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Summary statistics for the tree.\"\"\"\n        active = [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.status == \"active\"]\n        exhausted = [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.status == \"exhausted\"]\n        pruned = [n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.status == \"pruned\"]\n        best = min(\n            (n for n in self.nodes.values() if n.best_result_us is not None),\n            key=lambda n: n.best_result_us,  # type: ignore[arg-type]\n            default=None,\n        )\n        return {\n            \"kernel\": self.kernel_name,\n            \"total_nodes\": len(self.nodes),\n            \"active\": len(active),\n            \"exhausted\": len(exhausted),\n            \"pruned\": len(pruned),\n            \"best_us\": best.best_result_us if best else None,\n            \"best_strategy\": best.strategy if best else None,\n            \"total_attempts\": sum(n.attempts for n in self.nodes.values()),\n        }\n\n    # --- Persistence ---\n\n    def save(self, path: Path) -> None:\n        data = {\n            \"kernel_name\": self.kernel_name,\n            \"root_id\": self.root_id,\n            \"nodes\": {nid: asdict(n) for nid, n in self.nodes.items()},\n        }\n        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        path.write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2))\n\n    @classmethod\n    def load(cls, path: Path) -> KSearchTree:\n        data = json.loads(path.read_text())\n        tree = cls(data[\"kernel_name\"])\n        tree.root_id = data[\"root_id\"]\n        for nid, ndata in data[\"nodes\"].items():\n            tree.nodes[nid] = KNode(**ndata)\n        return tree\n\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\n        stats = self.get_stats()\n        return (\n            f\"KSearchTree({self.kernel_name}: \"\n            f\"{stats['active']} active, {stats['total_attempts']} attempts, \"\n            f\"best={stats['best_us']}µs)\"\n        )\n"
}

> AGENT

Now the templates. These are the core — they parameterize the submission.py files so the K-Search tree can explore configurations by varying JSON parameters rather than rewriting code.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Template Design**: We use Python's `string.Template` with `$PARAM` substitution rather than f-strings or Jinja2. Why? The generated code contains `{` and `}` everywhere (dict literals, format strings), so `$` substitution avoids escaping nightmares. The K-Search tree generates a JSON parameter dict, the generator substitutes it into the template, and we get a valid submission.py.

**Per-shape lookup tables**: The biggest win is moving from "one KSPLIT for all shapes" to "a lookup table mapping each (E, d_expert, bs) shape to its optimal KSPLIT." The geomean rewards improving the worst shapes disproportionately.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

...}\n  default_ksplit: str, fallback KSPLIT when shape not in table (default \"0\")\n  use_bypass: bool, set AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG=1 (default True)\n  use_opus: bool, set AITER_USE_NT=1 (default True)\n\"\"\"\n\nTEMPLATE = '''\\\nimport os,torch\nfrom task import input_t,output_t\nfrom aiter.fused_moe import fused_moe as fm\nfrom aiter import ActivationType as at,QuantType as qt\n\nos.environ[\"AITER_USE_NT\"]=\"$USE_NT\"\n_ks_table=$KSPLIT_TABLE\n_default_ks=\"$DEFAULT_KSPLIT\"\n_last_k=None\n\ndef _set_ksplit(e, d, bs):\n    global _last_k\n    key=f\"{e}_{d}_{bs}\"\n    ks=str(_ks_table.get(key, _default_ks))\n    if _last_k==ks:\n        return\n    _last_k=ks\n    if ks==\"0\":\n        os.environ.pop(\"AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG\",None)\n        os.environ.pop(\"AITER_KSPLIT\",None)\n    else:\n        os.environ[\"AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG\"]=\"1\"\n        os.environ[\"AITER_KSPLIT\"]=ks\n\ndef custom_kernel(data:input_t)->output_t:\n    hs,w1,w2,w1s,w2s,w1sh,w2sh,w1ssh,w2ssh,tw,ti,cfg=data\n    ne=w1sh.shape[0]\n    em=ti.numel()//ne\n    _set_ksplit(ne, cfg[\"d_expert\"], cfg[\"bs\"])\n    return fm(hs,w1sh,w2sh,tw,ti,expert_mask=None,activation=at.Silu,quant_type=qt.per_1x32,doweight_stage1=False,w1_scale=w1ssh,w2_scale=w2ssh,hidden_pad=cfg[\"d_hidden_pad\"]-cfg[\"d_hidden\"],intermediate_pad=cfg[\"d_expert_pad\"]-cfg[\"d_expert\"])\n'''\n\n# Default parameters for the template\nDEFAULT_PARAMS = {\n    \"USE_NT\": \"1\",\n    \"KSPLIT_TABLE\": {\n        \"257_256_16\": 4,\n        \"257_256_128\": 2,\n        \"257_256_512\": 0,\n   "content": "\"\"\"GEMM submission template.\n\nParameters (JSON):\n  kernel_table: dict mapping \"M_N_K\" -> {\"kernel\": str, \"log2_ks\": int}\n                e.g. {\"4_2880_512\": {\"kernel\": \"gemm_a4w4_asm\", \"log2_ks\": 2}}\n  default_kernel: str, fallback kernel name (default \"gemm_a4w4\")\n  default_log2_ks: int, fallback split-K (default 0)\n\"\"\"\n\nTEMPLATE = '''\\\nimport torch\nfrom task import input_t, output_t\nfrom aiter import dtypes\nimport aiter\nfrom aiter.ops.triton.quant import dynamic_mxfp4_quant\nfrom aiter.utility.fp4_utils import e8m0_shuffle\n\n_kt=$KERNEL_TABLE\n_dk=\"$DEFAULT_KERNEL\"\n_dks=$DEFAULT_LOG2_KS\n\ndef custom_kernel(data: input_t) -> output_t:\n    A, B, B_q, B_shuffle, B_scale_sh = data\n    A = A.contiguous()\n    M, K = A.shape\n    N = B_shuffle.shape[0]\n    key = f\"{M}_{N}_{K}\"\n    cfg = _kt.get(key, {\"kernel\": _dk, \"log2_ks\": _dks})\n    kname = cfg[\"kernel\"]\n    log2_ks = cfg[\"log2_ks\"]\n\n    A_q_raw, A_scale_raw = dynamic_mxfp4_quant(A)\n    A_scale_shuffled = e8m0_shuffle(A_scale_raw).view(dtypes.fp8_e8m0)\n    A_q = A_q_raw.view(dtypes.fp4x2)\n\n    gemm_fn = getattr(aiter, kname, aiter.gemm_a4w4)\n    if log2_ks > 0:\n        C = gemm_fn(\n            A_q, B_shuffle, A_scale_shuffled, B_scale_sh,\n            dtype=dtypes.bf16, bpreshuffle=True, log2_ks=log2_ks,\n        )\n    else:\n        C = gemm_fn(\n            A_q, B_shuffle, A_scale_shuffled, B_scale_sh,\n            dtype=dtypes.bf16, bpreshuffle=True,\n        )\n    return C\n'''\n\nDEFAULT_PARAMS = {\n    \"KERNEL_TABLE\": {\n        \"4_2880_512\": {\"kernel\": \"gemm_a4w4\", \"log2_ks\": 0},\n        \"16_2112_7168\": {\"kernel\": \"gemm_a4w4\", \"log2_ks\": 0},\n        \"32_4096_512\": {\"kernel\": \"gemm_a4w4\", \"log2_ks\": 0},\n        \"32_2880_512\": {\"kernel\": \"gemm_a4w4\", \"log2_ks\": 0},\n        \"64_7168_2048\": {\"kernel\": \"gemm_a4w4\", \"log2_ks\": 0},\n        \"256_3072_1536\": {\"kernel\": \"gemm_a4w4\", \"log2_ks\": 0},\n    },\n    \"DEFAULT_KERNEL\": \"gemm_a4w4\",\n    \"DEFAULT_LOG2_KS\": 0,\n}\n\n# Benchmark shapes from task.yml\nSHAPES = [\n    {\"M\": 4, \"N\": 2880, \"K\": 512},\n    {\"M\": 16, \"N\": 2112, \"K\": 7168},\n    {\"M\": 32, \"N\": 4096, \"K\": 512},\n    {\"M\": 32, \"N\": 2880, \"K\": 512},\n   "content": "\"\"\"MLA submission template.\n\nParameters (JSON):\n  splits_table: dict mapping \"bs_kvseqlen\" -> num_kv_splits (int)\n                e.g. {\"4_1024\": 4, \"4_8192\": 16, \"32_1024\": 8, ...}\n  default_splits: int, fallback num_kv_splits (default 16)\n  kv_format: \"mxfp4\" | \"fp8\" — which KV cache format to use\n  fast_mode: bool — aiter fast_mode metadata flag (default False)\n  kv_granularity: int — granularity for metadata (default 16)\n  use_persistent: bool — use ASM persistent kernel (default True)\n\"\"\"\n\nTEMPLATE = '''\\\nimport os,torch,ctypes\nfrom task import input_t,output_t\nfrom aiter import dtypes as dt\nimport aiter\n\nos.environ[\"AITER_MLA_USE_PERSISTENT\"]=\"1\"\nos.environ[\"AITER_USE_NT\"]=\"1\"\nos.environ[\"AITER_BYPASS_TUNE_CONFIG\"]=\"1\"\n\n_splits_table=$SPLITS_TABLE\n_default_splits=$DEFAULT_SPLITS\n_fast_mode=$FAST_MODE\n_kv_gran=$KV_GRANULARITY\n_kv_format=\"$KV_FORMAT\"\n_c,_o,_f1,_f2={},{},None,None\n\ndef _ea():\n    global _f1,_f2\n    if _f1:return\n    try:\n        from aiter.mla import mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd as f1,mla_reduce_v1 as f2\n        _f1,_f2=f1,f2\n    except:\n        import aiter as a\n        _f1,_f2=getattr(a,\"mla_decode_stage1_asm_fwd\",None),getattr(a,\"mla_reduce_v1\",None)\n\ndef _bm(b,q,n,qd,kd,qi,ki,ns):\n    from aiter import get_mla_metadata_info_v1 as gi,get_mla_metadata_v1 as gv\n    i=gi(b,q,n,qd,kd,is_sparse=False,fast_mode=_fast_mode,num_kv_splits=ns,intra_batch_mode=True)\n    w=[torch.empty(s,dtype=t,device=\"cuda\") for s,t in i]\n    wm,wi,ws,ri,rf,rp=w\n    kl=(ki[1:]-ki[:-1]).to(torch.int32)\n    gv(qi,ki,kl,n,1,True,wm,ws,wi,ri,rf,rp,page_size=1,kv_granularity=_kv_gran,max_seqlen_qo=q,uni_seqlen_qo=q,fast_mode=_fast_mode,max_split_per_batch=ns,intra_batch_mode=True,dtype_q=qd,dtype_kv=kd)\n    return {\"wm\":wm,\"wi\":wi,\"ws\":ws,\"ri\":ri,\"rf\":rf,\"rp\":rp,\"kl\":kl,\"ns\":ns}\n\ndef custom_kernel(data:input_t)->output_t:\n    q,kd,qi,ki,cfg=data\n    b,sl,nh=cfg[\"batch_size\"],cfg[\"kv_seq_len\"],cfg[\"num_heads\"]\n    _ea()\n    key=f\"{b}_{sl}\"\n    if key not in _c:\n        ns=_splits_table.get(key, _default_splits)\n        kv_dt=dt.fp4x2 if _kv_format==\"mxfp4\" else torch.float8_e4m3fnuz\n        _c[key]=_bm(b,1,nh,torch.bfloat16,kv_dt,qi,ki,ns)\n    m=_c[key]\n    ok=(b*nh,512)\n    if ok not in _o:\n        _o[ok]=torch.empty((b,nh,512),dtype=torch.bfloat16,device=\"cuda\")\n    ot=_o[ok]\n    ns=m[\"ns\"]\n    if _kv_format==\"mxfp4\":\n        kf,ks=kd[\"mxfp4\"]\n        k4=kf.view(kf.shape[0],1,1,288)\n    else:\n        kf,ks=kd[\"fp8\"]\n        k4=kf.view(kf.shape[0],1,1,576)\n    if _f1 and _f2:\n        sd=torch.empty((b,ns,nh,520),dtype=torch.float32,device=\"cuda\")\n        sl_=torch.empty((b,ns,nh),dtype=torch.float32,device=\"cuda\")\n        _f1(q.view(b,nh,576),k4,qi,ki,torch.arange(b*sl,dtype=torch.int32,device=\"cuda\"),m[\"kl\"],None,m[\"wm\"],m[\"wi\"],m[\"ws\"],1,1,1,1.0/(576**.5),sd,sl_,ot,q_scale=None,kv_scale=ks)\n        _f2(sd,sl_,m[\"ri\"],m[\"rf\"],m[\"rp\"],1,ot)\n        return ot\n    from aiter.mla import mla_decode_fwd as mf\n    return mf(q,k4,ot,qi,ki,page_size=1,nhead_kv=1,sm_scale=1.0/(576**.5),q_scale=None,kv_scale=ks,num_kv_splits=ns,intra_batch_mode=True)\n'''\n\nDEFAULT_PARAMS = {\n    \"SPLITS_TABLE\": {\n        \"4_1024\": 4,\n        \"4_8192\": 16,\n        \"32_1024\": 8,\n        \"32_8192\": 32,\n        \"128_8192\": 32,\n    },\n    \"DEFAULT_SPLITS\": 16,\n    \"FAST_MODE\": False,\n    \"KV_GRANULARITY\": 16,\n    \"KV_FORMAT\": \"mxfp4\",\n}\n\n# Benchmark shapes from task.yml\nSHAPES = [\n    {\"bs\": 4, \"qseqlen\": 1, \"kvseqlen\": 1024, \"tp\": 4},\n    {\"bs\": 4, \"qseqlen\": 4, \"kvseqlen\": 8192, \"tp\": 4},\n    {\"bs\": 32, \"qseqlen\": 1, \"kvseqlen\": 8192, \"tp\": 8},\n    {\"bs\": 32, \"qseqlen\": 4, \"kvseqlen\": 1024, \"tp\": 8},\n    {\"bs\": 32, \"qseqlen\": 1, \"kvseqlen\": 1024, \"tp\": 4},\n    {\"bs\": 32, \"qseqlen\": 4, \"kvseqlen\": 8192, \"tp\": 4},\n    {\"bs\": 128, \"qseqlen\": 1, \"kvseqlen\": 8192, \"tp\": 8},\n    {\"bs\": 128, \"qseqlen\": 4, \"kvseqlen\": 8192, {
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/generator.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Template-based submission.py generator.\n\nTakes a KNode's parameters + template, produces a valid submission.py.\nUses string.Template $PARAM substitution to avoid {/} escaping issues.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport ast\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom string import Template\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom templates import moe_template, gemm_template, mla_template\n\nTEMPLATES = {\n    \"moe\": moe_template.TEMPLATE,\n    \"gemm\": gemm_template.TEMPLATE,\n    \"mla\": mla_template.TEMPLATE,\n}\n\nDEFAULTS = {\n    \"moe\": moe_template.DEFAULT_PARAMS,\n    \"gemm\": gemm_template.DEFAULT_PARAMS,\n    \"mla\": mla_template.DEFAULT_PARAMS,\n}\n\n\ndef generate_submission(\n    kernel: str,\n    parameters: dict[str, Any],\n    output_path: Path | None = None,\n) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Generate a submission.py from template + parameters.\n\n    Args:\n        kernel: \"moe\", \"gemm\", or \"mla\"\n        parameters: Overrides for template defaults\n        output_path: If provided, write the generated code to this file\n\n    Returns:\n        Generated Python source code\n    \"\"\"\n    if kernel not in TEMPLATES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown kernel: {kernel}. Expected: {list(TEMPLATES)}\")\n\n    # Merge defaults with overrides\n    params = {**DEFAULTS[kernel], **parameters}\n\n    # Convert Python objects to repr strings for template substitution\n    subs = {}\n    for key, value in params.items():\n        if isinstance(value, (dict, list)):\n            subs[key] = json.dumps(value)\n        elif isinstance(value, bool):\n            subs[key] = str(value)\n        elif isinstance(value, (int, float)):\n            subs[key] = str(value)\n        else:\n            subs[key] = str(value)\n\n    template = Template(TEMPLATES[kernel])\n    code = template.safe_substitute(subs)\n\n    # Validate syntax\n    try:\n        ast.parse(code)\n    except SyntaxError as e:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Generated code has syntax error: {e}\\n---\\n{code}\") from e\n\n    if output_path:\n        output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        output_path.write_text(code)\n\n    return code\n\n\ndef generate_from_node(\n    kernel: str,\n    node_parameters: dict[str, Any],\n    output_dir: Path,\n) -> Path:\n    \"\"\"Generate submission.py from a K-Search node's parameters.\n\n    Returns path to generated file.\n    \"\"\"\n    output_path = output_dir / \"submission.py\"\n    generate_submission(kernel, node_parameters, output_path)\n    return output_path\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/evaluator.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Popcorn-cli wrapper for kernel evaluation.\n\nHandles test/benchmark/leaderboard submission modes,\noutput parsing for per-shape timings, and retry logic.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport re\nimport subprocess\nimport time\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\n\nPOPCORN_CLI = Path.home() / \".local\" / \"bin\" / \"popcorn-cli\"\nTIMEOUT_SECONDS = 720  # 12 min cap\n\n# Leaderboard names from popcorn-cli skill\nLEADERBOARD_NAMES = {\n    \"moe\": \"amd-moe-mxfp4\",\n    \"gemm\": \"amd-mxfp4-mm\",\n    \"mla\": \"amd-mixed-mla\",\n}\n\n# Kernel directory names (for submission path)\nKERNEL_DIRS = {\n    \"moe\": \"moe-mxfp4\",\n    \"gemm\": \"mxfp4-mm\",\n    \"mla\": \"mixed-mla\",\n}\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass EvalResult:\n    \"\"\"Result of a popcorn-cli evaluation.\"\"\"\n    kernel: str\n    mode: str  # test | benchmark | leaderboard\n    success: bool\n    geomean_us: float | None = None\n    per_shape_us: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    raw_output: str = \"\"\n    error: str = \"\"\n    duration_s: float = 0.0\n    timestamp: float = field(default_factory=time.time)\n\n    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        return {\n            \"kernel\": self.kernel,\n            \"mode\": self.mode,\n            \"success\": self.success,\n            \"geomean_us\": self.geomean_us,\n            \"per_shape_us\": self.per_shape_us,\n            \"error\": self.error,\n            \"duration_s\": self.duration_s,\n            \"timestamp\": self.timestamp,\n        }\n\n\ndef _parse_benchmark_output(output: str) -> tuple[float | None, dict[str, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Parse popcorn-cli benchmark output for timings.\n\n    Returns (geomean_us, {shape_key: time_us})\n    \"\"\"\n    per_shape: dict[str, float] = {}\n    geomean: float | None = None\n\n    # Look for timing lines like:\n    # Shape [M=4,N=2880,K=512]: 8.198 us\n    # or tabular format with us timings\n    for line in output.split(\"\\n\"):\n        # Match \"geomean\" or \"geometric mean\" lines\n        geo_match = re.search(r\"geo(?:metric)?[\\s_]*mean[:\\s]+([0-9.]+)\\s*(?:us|µs)\", line, re.IGNORECASE)\n        if geo_match:\n            geomean = float(geo_match.group(1))\n\n        # Match per-shape timing lines\n        shape_match = re.search(\n            r\"(?:Shape|Config|Test)\\s*\\[?([^\\]]+)\\]?\\s*[:=]\\s*([0-9.]+)\\s*(?:us|µs)\",\n            line, re.IGNORECASE\n        )\n        if shape_match:\n            shape_key = shape_match.group(1).strip()\n            per_shape[shape_key] = float(shape_match.group(2))\n\n        # Also match tabular format: \"  4  2880   512   8.198\"\n        tab_match = re.search(r\"^\\s*(\\d+)\\s+(\\d+)\\s+(\\d+)\\s+([0-9.]+)\\s*$\", line)\n        if tab_match:\n            shape_key = f\"{tab_match.group(1)}_{tab_match.group(2)}_{tab_match.group(3)}\"\n            per_shape[shape_key] = float(tab_match.group(4))\n\n    return geomean, per_shape\n\n\ndef _run_popcorn(\n    submission_path: Path,\n    kernel: str,\n    mode: str,\n    timeout: int = TIMEOUT_SECONDS,\n) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:\n    \"\"\"Run popcorn-cli submit command.\"\"\"\n    leaderboard = LEADERBOARD_NAMES[kernel]\n    cmd = [\n        str(POPCORN_CLI), \"submit\",\n        \"--no-tui\",\n        \"--mode\", mode,\n        \"--gpu\", \"MI355X\",\n        \"--leaderboard\", leaderboard,\n        str(submission_path),\n    ]\n    return subprocess.run(\n        cmd,\n        capture_output=True,\n        text=True,\n        timeout=timeout,\n    )\n\n\ndef evaluate(\n    submission_path: Path,\n    kernel: str,\n    mode: str = \"test\",\n    retry_on_artifact_failure: bool = True,\n) -> EvalResult:\n    \"\"\"Submit a kernel to popcorn-cli and parse results.\n\n    Args:\n        submission_path: Path to the submission.py file\n        kernel: \"moe\", \"gemm\", or \"mla\"\n        mode: \"test\", \"benchmark\", or \"leaderboard\"\n        retry_on_artifact_failure: Retry once on transient artifact errors\n\n    Returns:\n        EvalResult with parsed timings or error info\n    \"\"\"\n    if kernel not in LEADERBOARD_NAMES:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unknown kernel: {kernel}\")\n\n    start = time.time()\n\n    try:\n        result = _run_popcorn(submission_path, kernel, mode)\n        output = result.stdout + \"\\n\" + result.stderr\n        duration = time.time() - start\n\n        # Check for transient artifact failures\n        if retry_on_artifact_failure and \"artifact\" in output.lower() and result.returncode != 0:\n            time.sleep(5)\n            result = _run_popcorn(submission_path, kernel, mode)\n            output = result.stdout + \"\\n\" + result.stderr\n            duration = time.time() - start\n\n        if result.returncode != 0:\n            # Check for correctness errors\n            if \"mismatch\" in output.lower() or \"incorrect\" in output.lower():\n                return EvalResult(\n                    kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=False,\n                    raw_output=output, error=\"Correctness check failed\",\n                    duration_s=duration,\n                )\n            return EvalResult(\n                kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=False,\n                raw_output=output, error=f\"Exit code {result.returncode}\",\n                duration_s=duration,\n            )\n\n        geomean, per_shape = _parse_benchmark_output(output)\n        return EvalResult(\n            kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=True,\n            geomean_us=geomean, per_shape_us=per_shape,\n            raw_output=output, duration_s=duration,\n        )\n\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        return EvalResult(\n            kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=False,\n            error=f\"Timeout after {TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s\",\n            duration_s=time.time() - start,\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        return EvalResult(\n            kernel=kernel, mode=mode, success=False,\n            error=str(e),\n            duration_s=time.time() - start,\n        )\n\n\ndef test_then_benchmark(\n    submission_path: Path,\n    kernel: str,\n) -> EvalResult:\n    \"\"\"Run test first, then benchmark if test passes.\"\"\"\n    test_result = evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode=\"test\")\n    if not test_result.success:\n        return test_result\n    return evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode=\"benchmark\")\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/rate_limiter.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Rate limiter for popcorn-cli leaderboard submissions.\n\nEnforces 1 leaderboard submission per hour per problem.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n\nRATE_LIMIT_SECONDS = 3600  # 1 hour\n\n\nclass RateLimiter:\n    \"\"\"Track and enforce leaderboard submission rate limits.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, state_path: Path | None = None):\n        self.state_path = state_path or Path(__file__).parent / \"results\" / \"rate_limit_state.json\"\n        self.last_submission: dict[str, float] = {}\n        self._load()\n\n    def _load(self) -> None:\n        if self.state_path.exists():\n            self.last_submission = json.loads(self.state_path.read_text())\n\n    def _save(self) -> None:\n        self.state_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        self.state_path.write_text(json.dumps(self.last_submission, indent=2))\n\n    def can_submit(self, kernel: str) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Check if leaderboard submission is allowed for this kernel.\"\"\"\n        last = self.last_submission.get(kernel, 0.0)\n        return (time.time() - last) >= RATE_LIMIT_SECONDS\n\n    def seconds_until_allowed(self, kernel: str) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Seconds until next leaderboard submission is allowed.\"\"\"\n        last = self.last_submission.get(kernel, 0.0)\n        remaining = RATE_LIMIT_SECONDS - (time.time() - last)\n        return max(0.0, remaining)\n\n    def record_submission(self, kernel: str) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Record a leaderboard submission timestamp.\"\"\"\n        self.last_submission[kernel] = time.time()\n        self._save()\n\n    def status(self) -> dict[str, str]:\n        \"\"\"Human-readable status for each kernel.\"\"\"\n        result = {}\n        for kernel in [\"moe\", \"gemm\", \"mla\"]:\n            if self.can_submit(kernel):\n                result[kernel] = \"READY\"\n            else:\n                mins = self.seconds_until_allowed(kernel) / 60\n                result[kernel] = f\"WAIT {mins:.0f}m\"\n        return result\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/analyzer.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Result analysis → K-Search tree updates.\n\nAnalyzes benchmark results to compute priority updates,\nidentify bottleneck shapes, and suggest next experiments.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport math\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom evaluator import EvalResult\nfrom ksearch_tree import KSearchTree, KNode\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass Analysis:\n    \"\"\"Analysis of a benchmark result.\"\"\"\n    geomean_us: float\n    bottleneck_shape: str | None  # Shape contributing most to geomean\n    improvement_pct: float  # vs previous best (negative = regression)\n    per_shape_rank: dict[str, int]  # Rank of each shape (1 = worst)\n    suggestion: str  # Next experiment recommendation\n\n\ndef compute_geomean(timings: list[float]) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Geometric mean of timing values.\"\"\"\n    if not timings:\n        return float(\"inf\")\n    log_sum = sum(math.log(t) for t in timings if t > 0)\n    return math.exp(log_sum / len(timings))\n\n\ndef analyze_result(\n    result: EvalResult,\n    tree: KSearchTree,\n    node: KNode,\n) -> Analysis:\n    \"\"\"Analyze a benchmark result and update the K-Search tree.\n\n    Returns Analysis with insights and auto-updates the tree.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not result.success:\n        tree.mark_failed(node.id, result.error)\n        return Analysis(\n            geomean_us=float(\"inf\"),\n            bottleneck_shape=None,\n            improvement_pct=0.0,\n            per_shape_rank={},\n            suggestion=f\"Node failed: {result.error}. Try different parameters.\",\n        )\n\n    geomean = result.geomean_us or float(\"inf\")\n\n    # Update tree with result\n    tree.update_result(node.id, geomean)\n\n    # Find bottleneck shape (highest absolute time)\n    bottleneck = None\n    per_shape_rank: dict[str, int] = {}\n    if result.per_shape_us:\n        sorted_shapes = sorted(\n            result.per_shape_us.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True\n        )\n        per_shape_rank = {shape: i + 1 for i, (shape, _) in enumerate(sorted_shapes)}\n        bottleneck = sorted_shapes[0][0] if sorted_shapes else None\n\n    # Compute improvement vs tree's best\n    stats = tree.get_stats()\n    prev_best = stats.get(\"best_us\")\n    improvement = 0.0\n    if prev_best and prev_best > 0:\n        improvement = (prev_best - geomean) / prev_best * 100\n\n    # Generate suggestion\n    if geomean == float(\"inf\"):\n        suggestion = \"No valid timing. Check correctness.\"\n    elif bottleneck and result.per_shape_us:\n        worst_time = result.per_shape_us[bottleneck]\n        suggestion = (\n            f\"Focus on shape {bottleneck} ({worst_time:.1f}µs) — \"\n            f\"it's the bottleneck. Try varying parameters for this shape.\"\n        )\n    else:\n        suggestion = \"Try broader parameter sweep.\"\n\n    return Analysis(\n        geomean_us=geomean,\n        bottleneck_shape=bottleneck,\n        improvement_pct=improvement,\n        per_shape_rank=per_shape_rank,\n        suggestion=suggestion,\n    )\n\n\ndef log_result(\n    result: EvalResult,\n    node: KNode,\n    analysis: Analysis,\n    log_path: Path,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Append result to JSONL log file.\"\"\"\n    entry = {\n        \"node_id\": node.id,\n        \"strategy\": node.strategy,\n        \"parameters\": node.parameters,\n        **result.to_dict(),\n        \"analysis\": {\n            \"geomean_us\": analysis.geomean_us,\n            \"bottleneck_shape\": analysis.bottleneck_shape,\n            \"improvement_pct\": analysis.improvement_pct,\n            \"suggestion\": analysis.suggestion,\n        },\n    }\n    log_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(log_path, \"a\") as f:\n        f.write(json.dumps(entry) + \"\\n\")\n\n\ndef load_results(log_path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Load all results from JSONL log.\"\"\"\n    if not log_path.exists():\n        return []\n    results = []\n    for line in log_path.read_text().strip().split(\"\\n\"):\n        if line:\n            results.append(json.loads(line))\n    return results\n\n\ndef get_best_result(log_path: Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:\n    \"\"\"Get the best result (lowest geomean) from log.\"\"\"\n    results = load_results(log_path)\n    successful = [r for r in results if r.get(\"success\") and r.get(\"geomean_us\")]\n    if not successful:\n        return None\n    return min(successful, key=lambda r: r[\"geomean_us\"])\n"
}

shapes\",\n      \"parameters\": {},\n      \"parent_id\": null,\n      \"priority\": 1.0,\n      \"attempts\": 0,\n      \"best_result_us\": null,\n      \"stagnation_count\": 0,\n      \"status\": \"active\",\n      \"children\": [\"moe_n1\", \"moe_n2\", \"moe_n3\", \"moe_n4\", \"moe_n5\"],\n      \"notes\": \"\"\n    },\n    \"moe_n1\": {\n      \"id\": \"moe_n1\",\n      \"strategy\": \"Per-shape KSPLIT lookup table\",\n      \"parameters\": {\n        \"KSPLIT_TABLE\": {\n          \"257_256_16\": 4,\n          \"257_256_128\": 2,\n          \"257_256_512\": 0,\n          \"33_512_16\": 2,\n          \"33_512_128\": 2,\n          \"33_512_512\": 0,\n          \"33_2048_512\": 0\n        },\n        \"DEFAULT_KSPLIT\": \"2\"\n      },\n      \"parent_id\": \"root_moe\",\n      \"priority\": 0.95,\n      \"attempts\": 0,\n      \"best_result_us\": null,\n      \"stagnation_count\": 0,\n      \"status\": \"active\",\n      \"children\": [\"moe_n1a\", `★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Driver Design — the autonomous overnight loop**: This is the heart of the system. The key architectural choices:

1. **Priority-weighted rotation**: Instead of round-robin, we weight kernel selection by gap-to-leader (MoE 1.27x → highest priority since most closeable). This maximizes expected leaderboard improvement per cycle.

2. **Test-then-benchmark gating**: Every variant must pass correctness tests before benchmarking. This prevents wasting benchmark quota on broken code. The popcorn-cli `test` mode is free (no rate limit), only `leaderboard` is rate-limited to 1/hr/problem.

3. **JSONL logging**: Append-only, crash-safe. If the driver crashes at 3am, you don't lose the previous 20 experiments. Each line is self-contained JSON with all parameters, results, and tree state.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch/driver.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Autonomous K-Search experiment driver.\n\nMain overnight loop:\n  SELECT node → generate submission → popcorn-cli test → benchmark\n  → UPDATE tree → log JSONL → rotate kernels → repeat\n\nUsage:\n  python driver.py                    # Run all kernels (priority-weighted)\n  python driver.py --kernel moe       # Run only MoE experiments\n  python driver.py --kernel gemm      # Run only GEMM experiments\n  python driver.py --kernel mla       # Run only MLA experiments\n  python driver.py --max-cycles 10    # Limit number of cycles\n  python driver.py --dry-run          # Generate submissions but don't submit\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport random\nimport shutil\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom datetime import datetime\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\n# Add autoresearch to path\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))\n\nfrom ksearch_tree import KSearchTree\nfrom generator import generate_submission\nfrom evaluator import evaluate, test_then_benchmark, EvalResult, KERNEL_DIRS\nfrom analyzer import analyze_result, log_result, Analysis\nfrom rate_limiter import RateLimiter\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n    datefmt=\"%H:%M:%S\",\n)\nlog = logging.getLogger(\"autoresearch\")\n\nBASE_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\nTREE_DIR = BASE_DIR / \"tree\"\nRESULTS_DIR = BASE_DIR / \"results\"\nKERNELS_DIR = BASE_DIR.parent / \"kernels\"\n\n# Priority weights for kernel rotation (higher = more likely to be selected)\n# Based on gap-to-leader: MoE 1.27x (closeable), GEMM 2.4x, MLA 15.6x\nKERNEL_WEIGHTS = {\n    \"moe\": 0.5,   # Closest to leader — highest ROI\n    \"gemm\": 0.3,  # Medium gap\n    \"mla\": 0.2,   # Largest gap — moonshot\n}\n\nCYCLE_SLEEP_SECONDS = 5  # Sleep between cycles\n\n\ndef load_tree(kernel: str) -> KSearchTree:\n    \"\"\"Load or create K-Search tree for a kernel.\"\"\"\n    tree_path = TREE_DIR / f\"{kernel}_tree.json\"\n    if tree_path.exists():\n        return KSearchTree.load(tree_path)\n    tree = KSearchTree(kernel)\n    tree.insert_root(f\"Optimize {kernel} geomean\")\n    return tree\n\n\ndef save_tree(tree: KSearchTree) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Persist tree to disk.\"\"\"\n    tree_path = TREE_DIR / f\"{tree.kernel_name}_tree.json\"\n    tree.save(tree_path)\n\n\ndef select_kernel(kernels: list[str]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Weighted random selection of next kernel to optimize.\"\"\"\n    if len(kernels) == 1:\n        return kernels[0]\n    weights = [KERNEL_WEIGHTS.get(k, 0.33) for k in kernels]\n    total = sum(weights)\n    weights = [w / total for w in weights]\n    return random.choices(kernels, weights=weights, k=1)[0]\n\n\ndef generate_variant_params(tree: KSearchTree, node) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Generate a parameter variant from the node.\n\n    If the node has been attempted before, slightly perturb parameters\n    to explore nearby configurations.\n    \"\"\"\n    params = dict(node.parameters)\n\n    if node.attempts == 0:\n        return params  # First attempt uses exact parameters\n\n    # Perturbation: modify one random value in a lookup table\n    kernel = tree.kernel_name\n\n    if kernel == \"moe\" and \"KSPLIT_TABLE\" in params:\n        table = dict(params[\"KSPLIT_TABLE\"])\n        if table:\n            key = random.choice(list(table.keys()))\n            current = table[key]\n            # Perturb KSPLIT by ±1-2, clamped to 0-6\n            delta = random.choice([-2, -1, 1, 2])\n            table[key] = max(0, min(6, current + delta))\n            params[\"KSPLIT_TABLE\"] = table\n\n    elif kernel == \"gemm\" and \"KERNEL_TABLE\" in params:\n        table = dict(params[\"KERNEL_TABLE\"])\n        if table:\n            key = random.choice(list(table.keys()))\n            entry = dict(table[key])\n            # Perturb log2_ks by ±1, clamped to 0-4\n            delta = random.choice([-1, 1])\n            entry[\"log2_ks\"] = max(0, min(4, entry[\"log2_ks\"] + delta))\n            # Occasionally try ASM variant\n            if random.random() < 0.3:\n                entry[\"kernel\"] = random.choice([\"gemm_a4w4\", \"gemm_a4w4_asm\"])\n            table[key] = entry\n            params[\"KERNEL_TABLE\"] = table\n\n    elif kernel == \"mla\" and \"SPLITS_TABLE\" in params:\n        table = dict(params[\"SPLITS_TABLE\"])\n        if table:\n            key = random.choice(list(table.keys()))\n            current = table[key]\n            # Perturb splits: ×2, ÷2, ±4\n            choices = [\n                max(2, current // 2),\n                min(64, current * 2),\n                max(2, current - 4),\n                min(64, current + 4),\n            ]\n            table[key] = random.choice(choices)\n            params[\"SPLITS_TABLE\"] = table\n\n    return params\n\n\ndef run_cycle(\n    kernel: str,\n    tree: KSearchTree,\n    rate_limiter: RateLimiter,\n    dry_run: bool = False,\n) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"Run one experiment cycle for a kernel.\n\n    Returns (success, summary_message).\n    \"\"\"\n    # Select best node\n    node = tree.select_best()\n    if node is None:\n        return False, f\"{kernel}: No active nodes remaining\"\n\n    log.info(f\"[{kernel}] Selected: {node.strategy} (p={node.priority:.2f}, attempts={node.attempts})\")\n\n    # Generate variant parameters\n    params = generate_variant_params(tree, node)\n\n    # Generate submission\n    try:\n        code = generate_submission(kernel, params)\n    except ValueError as e:\n        log.error(f\"[{kernel}] Generation failed: {e}\")\n        tree.mark_failed(node.id, str(e))\n        save_tree(tree)\n        return False, f\"{kernel}: Generation failed: {e}\"\n\n    if dry_run:\n        log.info(f\"[{kernel}] DRY RUN — would submit {len(code)} chars\")\n        return True, f\"{kernel}: Dry run OK ({len(code)} chars)\"\n\n    # Write submission to kernel directory\n    kernel_dir = KERNELS_DIR / KERNEL_DIRS[kernel]\n    staging_dir = kernel_dir / \"staging\"\n    staging_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    # Save to staging with timestamp\n    ts = datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y%m%d_%H%M%S\")\n    staged_path = staging_dir / f\"submission.autoresearch.{ts}.py\"\n    staged_path.write_text(code)\n\n    # Copy to submission.py for popcorn-cli\n    submission_path = kernel_dir / \"submission.py\"\n    # Back up current submission\n    backup_path = staging_dir / f\"submission.backup.{ts}.py\"\n    if submission_path.exists():\n        shutil.copy2(submission_path, backup_path)\n    submission_path.write_text(code)\n\n    # Test first, then benchmark\n    log.info(f\"[{kernel}] Testing...\")\n    test_result = evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode=\"test\")\n\n    if not test_result.success:\n        log.warning(f\"[{kernel}] Test FAILED: {test_result.error}\")\n        tree.mark_failed(node.id, f\"Test failed: {test_result.error}\")\n        # Restore backup\n        if backup_path.exists():\n            shutil.copy2(backup_path, submission_path)\n        save_tree(tree)\n        # Log failure\n        log_result(\n            test_result, node,\n            Analysis(float(\"inf\"), None, 0.0, {}, test_result.error),\n            RESULTS_DIR / f\"{kernel}_runs.jsonl\",\n        )\n        return False, f\"{kernel}: Test failed: {test_result.error}\"\n\n    log.info(f\"[{kernel}] Test PASSED. Benchmarking...\")\n    bench_result = evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode=\"benchmark\")\n\n    if not bench_result.success:\n        log.warning(f\"[{kernel}] Benchmark FAILED: {bench_result.error}\")\n        # Still update tree (test passed, benchmark failed is different from correctness failure)\n        if backup_path.exists():\n            shutil.copy2(backup_path, submission_path)\n        save_tree(tree)\n        return False, f\"{kernel}: Benchmark failed: {bench_result.error}\"\n\n    # Analyze and update tree\n    analysis = analyze_result(bench_result, tree, node)\n    save_tree(tree)\n\n    # Log result\n    log_result(bench_result, node, analysis, RESULTS_DIR / f\"{kernel}_runs.jsonl\")\n\n    geomean = bench_result.geomean_us or 0\n    log.info(\n        f\"[{kernel}] Geomean: {geomean:.1f}µs \"\n        f\"(improvement: {analysis.improvement_pct:+.1f}%) \"\n        f\"Bottleneck: {analysis.bottleneck_shape}\"\n    )\n\n    # If this is the best result AND leaderboard is available, consider submitting\n    stats = tree.get_stats()\n    if (\n        stats[\"best_us\"] == geomean\n        and analysis.improvement_pct > 0\n        and rate_limiter.can_submit(kernel)\n    ):\n        log.info(f\"[{kernel}] NEW BEST! Submitting to leaderboard...\")\n        lb_result = evaluate(submission_path, kernel, mode=\"leaderboard\")\n        rate_limiter.record_submission(kernel)\n        if lb_result.success:\n            log.info(f\"[{kernel}] Leaderboard submission OK!\")\n        else:\n            log.warning(f\"[{kernel}] Leaderboard submission failed: {lb_result.error}\")\n    else:\n        # Restore backup if not best\n        if backup_path.exists() and stats[\"best_us\"] != geomean:\n            shutil.copy2(backup_path, submission_path)\n\n    summary = (\n        f\"{kernel}: {geomean:.1f}µs \"\n        f\"(best={stats['best_us']:.1f}µs, \"\n        f\"{stats['active']} active nodes, \"\n        f\"{stats['total_attempts']} total attempts)\"\n    )\n    return True, summary\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"K-Search autonomous experiment driver\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--kernel\", choices=[\"moe\", \"gemm\", \"mla\"], help=\"Single kernel to optimize\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--max-cycles\", type=int, default=0, help=\"Max experiment cycles (0=unlimited)\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--dry-run\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Generate but don't submit\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    kernels = [args.kernel] if args.kernel else [\"moe\", \"gemm\", \"mla\"]\n    rate_limiter = RateLimiter()\n\n    log.info(f\"Starting K-Search driver for: {', '.join(kernels)}\")\n    log.info(f\"Max cycles: {'unlimited' if args.max_cycles == 0 else args.max_cycles}\")\n    log.info(f\"Rate limiter: {rate_limiter.status()}\")\n\n    # Load trees\n    trees = {k: load_tree(k) for k in kernels}\n    for k, t in trees.items():\n        log.info(f\"  {k}: {t}\")\n\n    cycle = 0\n    try:\n        while args.max_cycles == 0 or cycle < args.max_cycles:\n            cycle += 1\n            kernel = select_kernel(kernels)\n            tree = trees[kernel]\n\n            log.info(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n            log.info(f\"Cycle {cycle}: {kernel}\")\n            log.info(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n            success, summary = run_cycle(kernel, tree, rate_limiter, dry_run=args.dry_run)\n            log.info(summary)\n\n            # Periodic priority decay to encourage exploration\n            if cycle % 5 == 0:\n                for t in trees.values():\n                    t.decay_priorities(factor=0.95)\n                    save_tree(t)\n\n            time.sleep(CYCLE_SLEEP_SECONDS)\n\n    except KeyboardInterrupt:\n        log.info(\"\\nShutting down...\")\n    finally:\n        # Save all trees\n        for t in trees.values():\n            save_tree(t)\n        # Print final stats\n        log.info(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        log.info(f\"Final stats after {cycle} cycles:\")\n        for k, t in trees.items():\n            stats = t.get_stats()\n            log.info(\n                f\"  {k}: best={stats['best_us']}µs, \"\n                f\"active={stats['active']}, \"\n                f\"attempts={stats['total_attempts']}\"\n            )\n        log.info(f\"{'='*60}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

> AGENT

Phase 1 infrastructure is complete. Here's the summary:

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**What was built — 987 lines of autonomous optimization infrastructure:**

1. **`ksearch_tree.py` (175 lines)** — KNode dataclass + KSearchTree with SELECT (priority-weighted leaf selection), INSERT, UPDATE (with stagnation tracking), PRUNE (K=7 threshold), and JSON persistence. This is the "brain" that decides what to try next.

2. **3 parameterized templates** — Each kernel's `submission.py` is now a `string.Template` with `$PARAM` slots for per-shape lookup tables. The key innovation: instead of hardcoded values, each shape gets its own configuration via a lookup table keyed by `"E_dexpert_bs"` (MoE), `"M_N_K"` (GEMM), or `"bs_kvseqlen"` (MLA).

3. **`evaluator.py` (198 lines)** — Wraps `popcorn-cli submit` with test/benchmark/leaderboard modes, output parsing (regex-based to handle varying output formats), retry on transient failures, and 12-minute timeout.

4. **`driver.py` (327 lines)** — The autonomous loop. Priority-weighted kernel rotation (MoE 50%, GEMM 30%, MLA 20% reflecting gap-to-leader). Each cycle: SELECT → generate → test → benchmark → UPDATE → log. Automatic leaderboard submission on new bests (rate-limited to 1/hr/problem). Periodic priority decay for exploration.

5. **3 pre-seeded K-Search trees** — 36 total nodes encoding the search space from the plan. MoE: 11 nodes (KSPLIT tables, OPUS, bypass). GEMM: 11 nodes (per-shape kernel+split-K). MLA: 14 nodes (adaptive splits, FP8 vs MXFP4, fast_mode, kv_granularity).

**Architecture decision — why template substitution over LLM code generation**: The plan mentions "Ollama JSON params" but templates are faster, deterministic, and don't consume LLM tokens. Since the search space is finite (per-shape lookup tables with bounded parameter ranges), we don't need LLM creativity — we need exhaustive exploration. The K-Search tree handles the exploration strategy; templates handle the code generation. LLM generation can be added later for "escape" nodes that need novel code.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`

## Ready to Run

```bash
# Dry run (no popcorn-cli needed):
cd research/challenges/luma_amd_speedrun/autoresearch
python driver.py --dry-run --max-cycles 5

# Real run (needs popcorn-cli + MI355X runner):
python driver.py --kernel moe --max-cycles 20   # MoE sprint
python driver.py                                  # All kernels overnight
```

## What's Next (Phase 2-4)

The infrastructure is built. The next steps are execution:
1. **Establish baselines** — Submit current submission.py for all 3 kernels to get real per-shape timings
2. **MoE sprint** — Run `python driver.py --kernel moe --max-cycles 20` and let it sweep KSPLIT tables
3. **GEMM sprint** — Sweep kernel+split-K combinations per shape
4. **MLA moonshot** — Adaptive num_kv_splits is the biggest lever

We're missing two things: (1) a README for the autoresearch system, and (2) an update to COORDINATION.md registering autoresearch as a session. Let me create both.

`★ Insight ─────────────────────────────────────`
**Why documentation matters here**: This is a multi-session, multi-agent codebase with COORDINATION.md acting as a mutex protocol. If autoresearch runs overnight unattended, other sessions (opencode, gemini, antigravity) need to know it's active and may be modifying submission.py files. Without registering in COORDINATION.md, another session could overwrite autoresearch's best result, or two sessions could submit to the leaderboard simultaneously.
`─────────────────────────────────────────────────`
